60	def pythonw():
61	    cand = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(sys.executable), "pythonw.exe")
62	    return cand if os.path.exists(cand) else sys.executable
63	
64	
65	def running_cmdlines():
66	    """Command lines of every python process. [] if unreadable (then a duplicate is possible, never a kill)."""
67	    if os.name != "nt":
68	        return []
69	    try:
70	        r = subprocess.run(["powershell", "-NoProfile", "-Command",
71	                            "Get-CimInstance Win32_Process -Filter \"Name like 'python%'\" | "
72	                            "ForEach-Object { $_.CommandLine }"],
73	                           capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=60, creationflags=NO_WINDOW)
74	        return [l.strip() for l in r.stdout.splitlines() if l.strip()]
75	    except Exception:
76	        return []
77	
78	
79	def _key(args):
80	    """What identifies a job in a command line: the script path or the -m module, slash-agnostic."""
81	    a = args[1] if args[0] == "-m" else args[0]
82	    return a.replace("/", "\\").lower() if args[0] != "-m" else a.lower()
83	
84	
85	def is_running(args, cmdlines):
86	    k = _key(args)
87	    return any(k in c.replace("/", "\\").lower() for c in cmdlines)
88	
89	
90	def start(label, args, cmdlines=None, _popen=subprocess.Popen):
91	    """Start one job hidden unless it is already running. Logs free RAM with the decision. Returns bool."""
92	    cmdlines = running_cmdlines() if cmdlines is None else cmdlines
93	    if is_running(args, cmdlines):
94	        MG.log_launch(label, "already running - left alone")
95	        return False
96	    MG.log_launch(label, "START (hidden, pythonw)")
97	    out, err = _desk_logs(label)                          # R-NO-WINDOWS: output to E1-DESK\logs, never a console

def held():
    """R-HOLD-AND-FIX: True = every order path HOLDS (place nothing). Held while the manual flag exists OR
    AUDIT-3B has not passed. Fail-closed — anything unclear means held. Recorders/diaries/dashboards ignore
    this; only order writers call it."""
    return os.path.exists(HOLD_FLAG) or not audit3b_passed()

212	    """Parse START_E1_ALL.bat for the `pythonw scripts\\X.py [args]` jobs it launches. Skips REM-commented
213	    lines (a commented job is not a job) and RETIRED scripts (never resurrected)."""
214	    import re as _re
215	    jobs = []
216	    try:
217	        for ln in open(START_BAT, encoding="utf-8", errors="replace"):
218	            if ln.lstrip().upper().startswith("REM"):
219	                continue                                     # R-AUDIT-DAY1-FIX (2): a commented line is not a job

221	            if m and os.path.basename(m.group(1).strip()) not in RETIRED:

228	def _job_running(script_base):
229	    """True if a pythonw process is running that script (fail-safe True on error — never spam launches)."""
230	    try:
231	        out = subprocess.run(["powershell", "-NoProfile", "-Command",
232	                              "(Get-CimInstance Win32_Process -Filter \"Name='pythonw.exe'\" | "
233	                              "Where-Object { $_.CommandLine -like '*%s*' } | Measure-Object).Count" % script_base],
234	                             capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=15,
235	                             creationflags=CREATE_NO_WINDOW).stdout.strip()
236	        # Fail-safe against DUPLICATE launches (R-E1-FINAL-ARCH C2): only an explicit "0" means not running;
237	        # an EMPTY result (the CIM query timing out under heavy backtest load) is assumed RUNNING, never

32	def count_today(date=None, path=None):
33	    """How many rows carry today's date — for the close line 'Lead mistakes today: N'."""
34	    path = path or LEDGER
35	    date = date or _today()
36	    try:
37	        return sum(1 for ln in open(path, encoding="utf-8")
38	                   if ln.startswith("| " + date + " |"))
39	    except OSError:
40	        return 0

tool_result: 1	"""NB12 item 4 (item 44 PREPARE half) — decide whether to relaunch the build session on the next pending item,
2	and (when armed) do it in a hidden, single-instance window above the memory floor. NEW, un-wired.
3	Wire-in tonight = a watchdog call.
4	
5	DELIBERATE: the actual spawn is NOT implemented here. §14.7 (owner dispatch CHAIN-START-2) makes restart a HUMAN
6	act — a session that resurrects itself is exactly the ghost-runner the owner forbade. So this module ships only
7	the tested DECISION (single-instance + pending-work + memory-floor gates); the real relaunch is an owner/watchdog-
8	armed step supplied at wire-in via `launch_fn`, and the default raises rather than silently spawning autonomy.
9	The test injects a stub launcher.
10	"""
11	from __future__ import annotations
12	
13	import os
14	import time
15	
16	_REPO = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
17	OWNER_ACTIVE = os.path.join(_REPO, "scheduler", ".owner_active")   # §14.1 marker; a fresh one = a live session
18	FRESH_S = 4 * 3600                                                 # §14.1: a marker older than 4h is ignored
19	
20	
21	def _session_active(now=None, path=None):
22	    """True iff a build/owner session marker exists and is fresh (< 4h) — the single-instance guard."""
23	    path = path or OWNER_ACTIVE
24	    try:
25	        age = (now or time.time()) - os.path.getmtime(path)
26	        return age < FRESH_S
27	    except OSError:
28	        return False
29	
30	
31	def _ram_ok():
32	    import memory_guard
33	    return memory_guard.heavy_ok()
34	
35	
36	def _pending():
37	    import master_checklist
38	    return master_checklist.actionable()
39	
40	
41	def _launch(item):
42	    raise NotImplementedError(
43	        "relaunch is owner/watchdog-armed at wire-in (§14.7: restart is a human act) — inject a launch_fn")
44	
45	
46	def should_resurrect(session_active, pending, ram_ok):
47	    """Pure gate. Relaunch only when no session is live, there is pending work, and RAM is above the floor."""
48	    if session_active:
49	        return False, "a build session is already active (single-instance)"
50	    if not pending:
51	        return False, "no pending item to resume"
52	    if not ram_ok:
53	        return False, "free RAM below the memory floor"
54	    return True, "ok"
55	
56	
57	def resurrect(session_active_fn=_session_active, pending_fn=_pending, ram_ok_fn=_ram_ok, launch_fn=_launch):
58	    pending = pending_fn()
59	    ok, why = should_resurrect(session_active_fn(), pending, ram_ok_fn())
60	    if not ok:
61	        return {"launched": False, "reason": why}
62	    launch_fn(pending[0])
63	    return {"launched": True, "item": pending[0]}
64	
65	
66	def _demo():
67	    launched = []
68	    stub = lambda item: launched.append(item)
69	    # happy path: no session, work pending, RAM ok -> launches the first pending item
70	    r = resurrect(lambda: False, lambda: ["item 45", "item 7"], lambda: True, stub)
71	    assert r["launched"] is True and launched == ["item 45"], r
72	    # session live -> single-instance guard blocks
73	    assert resurrect(lambda: True, lambda: ["x"], lambda: True, stub)["launched"] is False
74	    # nothing pending -> no relaunch
75	    assert resurrect(lambda: False, lambda: [], lambda: True, stub)["launched"] is False
76	    # RAM below floor -> hold
77	    assert resurrect(lambda: False, lambda: ["x"], lambda: False, stub)["launched"] is False
78	    assert launched == ["item 45"]                        # only the happy path ever launched
79	    # the real launcher refuses to silently spawn autonomy
80	    try:
81	        _launch("x"); assert False, "default launch should refuse"
82	    except NotImplementedError:
83	        pass
84	    print("session_resurrect demo OK: launches only when idle+pending+RAM-ok; default spawn refuses (§14.7).")
85	
86	
87	if __name__ == "__main__":
88	    import sys
89	    if "--demo" in sys.argv:
90	        _demo()
91

243	def ready_line(s=None):
244	    """R-VITALS: the 08:30 READY line derived from the status file. Login + a green room = READY."""
245	    s = s or build(write=False)
246	    if not s["token"].get("valid"):
247	        return "NOT READY YET — waiting for the login."
248	    recorders = sum(1 for p in s["processes"] if p["name"] in
249	                    ("e13_depth", "chain_recorder", "xchange_recorder", "positioning_recorder"))
250	    runner = any(p["name"] == "book20_runner" for p in s["processes"])
251	    if recorders >= 1 and runner and s["moment_light_green_count"] > 0:
252	        return "READY — %d recorders + runner up, %d stocks ready, orders %s." % (
253	            recorders, s["moment_light_green_count"], "allowed" if s["orders_allowed_now"] else "arming")
254	    return "NOT READY — %s" % (s.get("plain") or _plain(s))
